# Goal

I want you to fix bugs and increase the score toward a target for a Kaggle competition solution. Here is the information you need.

# Requirements

- Keep changes minimal unless necessary.
- Preserve the core logic, including model architecture, layers, training approach/loops, feature extraction, or loss function. Maintain identical core logic and evaluation semantics; only allow negligible floating-point differences.
- Every change must be directly relevant to the stated issue (big fix and/or evaluation score improvement); avoid unrelated refactors or stylistic edits.
- Do NOT introduce extra approximations, sampling, reduced precision, early stopping, or relaxed convergence criteria.
- Ensure it runs end-to-end and produces a valid submission file.


# 1. Kaggle task description

## Task
Create a classifier to predict whether an image contains a cactus.

## Metric
Area under the ROC curve.

## Submission Format
For each ID in the test set, you must predict a probability for the `has_cactus` variable. The file should contain a header and have the following format:

```
id,has_cactus
000940378805c44108d287872b2f04ce.jpg,0.5
0017242f54ececa4512b4d7937d1e21e.jpg,0.5
001ee6d8564003107853118ab87df407.jpg,0.5
etc.
```

## Dataset
This dataset contains a large number of 32 x 32 thumbnail images containing aerial photos of a cactus. The file name of an image corresponds to its `id`.

- **train/** - the training set images
- **test/** - the test set images (you must predict the labels of these)
- **train.csv** - the training set labels, indicates whether the image has a cactus (`has_cactus = 1`)
- **sample_submission.csv** - a sample submission file in the correct format

## === cell 0
import os
import random
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import cv2

import tf_keras as keras

SEED = 42
os.environ["PYTHONHASHSEED"] = str(SEED)
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
try:
    keras.utils.set_random_seed(SEED)
except Exception:
    pass

## === cell 1
BASE = "/kaggle/input/aerial-cactus-identification"
TRAIN_CSV = f"{BASE}/train.csv"
SAMPLE_SUB = f"{BASE}/sample_submission.csv"

TRAIN_DIR = f"{BASE}/train"
TEST_DIR = f"{BASE}/test"

train_label = pd.read_csv(TRAIN_CSV)
submission = pd.read_csv(SAMPLE_SUB)

list_0, list_1 = [], []
for i in train_label["has_cactus"].values:
    if i == 0:
        list_0.append(i)
    else:
        list_1.append(i)

train_images, train_labels, test_images = [], [], []

for i in train_label["has_cactus"].values:
    train_labels.append(i)

train_file = os.listdir(TRAIN_DIR)
train_file.sort()
test_file = os.listdir(TEST_DIR)
test_file.sort()

if len(train_file) == 0:
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"No training images found in {TRAIN_DIR}. Check dataset paths."
    )
if len(test_file) == 0:
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"No test images found in {TEST_DIR}. Check dataset paths.")

for image_name in train_file:
    img = cv2.imread(os.path.join(TRAIN_DIR, image_name))
    if img is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Failed to read train image: {image_name}")
    train_images.append(img)

for image_name in test_file:
    img = cv2.imread(os.path.join(TEST_DIR, image_name))
    if img is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Failed to read test image: {image_name}")
    test_images.append(img)

train_images = np.array(train_images, dtype=np.float32) / 255.0
train_labels = np.array(train_labels, dtype=np.float32)
test_images = np.array(test_images, dtype=np.float32) / 255.0

if train_images.shape[0] != train_labels.shape[0]:
    raise ValueError(
        f"Mismatch: {train_images.shape[0]} train images but {train_labels.shape[0]} labels. "
        f"TRAIN_DIR={TRAIN_DIR}, TRAIN_CSV={TRAIN_CSV}"
    )

train_images.shape, train_labels.shape, test_images.shape

## === cell 2
from tf_keras.models import Sequential
from tf_keras.layers import (
    Dense,
    Flatten,
    Dropout,
    Conv2D,
    MaxPooling2D,
    LeakyReLU,
    BatchNormalization,
)
from tf_keras.optimizers import Adam
from tf_keras.callbacks import LearningRateScheduler, ModelCheckpoint

model = Sequential()
model.add(Conv2D(64, (3, 3), padding="same", input_shape=(32, 32, 3)))
model.add(BatchNormalization(momentum=0.5, epsilon=1e-5, gamma_initializer="uniform"))
model.add(LeakyReLU(alpha=0.1))
model.add(Conv2D(64, (3, 3), padding="same"))
model.add(BatchNormalization(momentum=0.1, epsilon=1e-5, gamma_initializer="uniform"))

model.add(LeakyReLU(alpha=0.1))
model.add(MaxPooling2D(2, 2))
model.add(Dropout(0.2))

model.add(Conv2D(128, (3, 3), padding="same"))
model.add(BatchNormalization(momentum=0.2, epsilon=1e-5, gamma_initializer="uniform"))
model.add(LeakyReLU(alpha=0.1))
model.add(Conv2D(128, (3, 3), padding="same"))
model.add(BatchNormalization(momentum=0.1, epsilon=1e-5, gamma_initializer="uniform"))

model.add(LeakyReLU(alpha=0.1))
model.add(MaxPooling2D(2, 2))
model.add(Dropout(0.2))

model.add(Conv2D(256, (3, 3), padding="same"))
model.add(BatchNormalization(momentum=0.2, epsilon=1e-5, gamma_initializer="uniform"))
model.add(LeakyReLU(alpha=0.1))

model.add(Conv2D(128, (3, 3), padding="same"))
model.add(BatchNormalization(momentum=0.1, epsilon=1e-5, gamma_initializer="uniform"))
model.add(LeakyReLU(alpha=0.1))

model.add(MaxPooling2D(2, 2))
model.add(Dropout(0.2))

model.add(Flatten())
model.add(Dense(256, activation="relu", name="dense1"))
model.add(LeakyReLU(alpha=0.1))
model.add(BatchNormalization())
model.add(Dense(1, activation="sigmoid"))




## === cell 3
initial_learningrate = 1e-3


def lr_decay(epoch):
    return initial_learningrate * (0.99**epoch)


ckpt_path = "/kaggle/working/best_model.h5"
os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(ckpt_path), exist_ok=True)

checkpoint = ModelCheckpoint(
    filepath=ckpt_path,
    monitor="val_loss",
    verbose=1,
    save_best_only=True,
    save_weights_only=False,
)

model.compile(
    loss="binary_crossentropy",
    optimizer=Adam(learning_rate=initial_learningrate),
    metrics=["acc"],
)

history = model.fit(
    train_images,
    train_labels,
    epochs=100,
    batch_size=128,
    callbacks=[LearningRateScheduler(lr_decay, verbose=1), checkpoint],
    validation_split=0.2,
    verbose=1,
)

## === cell 4
from tf_keras.models import load_model

if os.path.exists(ckpt_path):
    best_model = load_model(ckpt_path)
else:
    best_model = model  # fallback to avoid FileNotFoundError

pred = best_model.predict(test_images, verbose=1).reshape(-1)
pred = np.clip(pred, 0.0, 1.0)

test_ids = [fn for fn in test_file]  # sorted already
id_to_pred = dict(zip(test_ids, pred))

sub_df = submission.copy()
sub_df["has_cactus"] = sub_df["id"].map(id_to_pred)

if sub_df["has_cactus"].isna().any():
    sub_df["has_cactus"] = sub_df["has_cactus"].fillna(float(np.mean(pred)))

sub_df.to_csv("submission.csv", index=False)

print(sub_df.head())
print("Wrote:", os.path.abspath("submission.csv"), "rows:", len(sub_df))
print("Pred stats:", float(np.min(pred)), float(np.mean(pred)), float(np.max(pred)))
